- **2026-08-09T22:55Z — MONET — COMPLETED — Senate 5-year historical backfill: relay-reliability root cause #2 found + fixed (Docker bridge hairpin NAT), sweep run to completion.** Follows the earlier `monet/senate-relay-env-fix` (#1602, merged/deployed — fixed a real bug: `process.env.SENATE_RELAY_URL` was unreliable in this Deno runtime, callers now thread `env.SENATE_RELAY_URL` explicitly). After that deploy, re-verification STILL showed intermittent full timeouts (client `000`/524) — but this time root-caused precisely, not left as "the WAF is flaky": `docker exec`'d directly into the prod container and ran plain `curl` from inside it to the SSH-reverse-tunnel/socat bridge address (`10.0.2.1:8900`, the docker bridge gateway) — hung 30s and failed with `fetch failed`, while the IDENTICAL curl run from the HOST (`ssh coolify curl ...`, not inside the container) succeeded in ~2s every time, and the container's own general internet egress (HTTPS to efdsearch.senate.gov, `ip route`, ping to the gateway) was all instant and healthy. This isolates a Docker bridge hairpin-NAT problem specific to container→gateway-IP→host-bound-port traffic (a known class of Docker networking edge case), not a Senate/Imperva/relay issue at all — the relay itself was healthy the whole time, verified by its own request log. **Fix**: replaced the SSH-reverse-tunnel + socat bridge path with an ephemeral Cloudflare quick tunnel (`cloudflared tunnel --url http://127.0.0.1:8899`, zero config, no shared fleet-infra touched — distinct from the existing named agent-sync tunnel, confirmed both coexist) exposing the same local relay over normal public HTTPS, which the container already proved it can reach reliably (container egress path, not the flaky bridge-gateway path). Verified 6+ consecutive container-side + full-production-edge round trips at ~1.5-2.2s each (vs. the old path's 100s+ hangs) before switching `SENATE_RELAY_URL` (Coolify env, redeployed) and tearing down the old tunnel/socat. Live (non-dry-run) month-by-month sweep 2021-01→2026-08 run to completion via `senate_5yr_sweep.sh` against the real admin endpoint (all 68 months). **Result**: 57/68 months succeeded on the first pass; the remaining 11 (2021-03, 2024-06, 2024-10→2025-05, 2026-03) hit transient failures from the ephemeral tunnel under sustained sequential load — all 11 succeeded cleanly on an immediate retry, zero unresolved errors. **Before → after** (live Senate transactions by filed year, prod DB, `deprecated_at IS NULL`): 2021 was ~0% official-source coverage per the earlier chamber×year scoping query; now 173 (2021) / 933 (2022) / 1089 (2023) / 746 (2024) / 1088 (2025) / 1217 (2026) — 5,246 real Senate PTR transactions across the 5-year window, all officially sourced via efdsearch.senate.gov through the relay, deduped against pre-existing data (979 total Senate filings in prod). **Follow-up filed**: #1604 (tunnel durability — accountless/ephemeral quick tunnel, no uptime guarantee, URL regenerates on restart, still depends on the owner's Mac staying on for as long as Senate 403s the box's own datacenter IP). **New finding filed separately while doing the final verification pass**: #1607 — House shows a real 2024-2025 coverage dip (2024: 1,894 tx vs ~8,200-8,400 in 2022/2023; 2025: fully absent/0 rows), with a correlated dip in Executive (2024: 9 tx vs 97 in 2023, 164 in 2025) — ruled out the known filed_date-NULL issue (checked: 0 House transactions have a NULL filed_date; the 985 NULL-filed_date House *filings* are 91% already-diagnosed phantom scout-probe `not_found` rows from the Lane-2 investigation, not this) — a distinct, not-yet-investigated gap, needs the same style of crawl-history root-cause dig just done for Senate. Not actioned in this lane; flagged and filed so it isn't lost.

- **2026-08-09 — MONET — IN PR (auto-merge enabled) — Trades-tab count accuracy (3 owner-reported bugs) + stable resizable table layout (`monet/trades-count-fix`).** Branch `monet/trades-count-fix` from `origin/main`; two slices landed on this one branch/PR. **Bug 1 (total drifted upward, +page-size per poll/screenshot):** `dashboardHtml.ts` `fetchUpdates()` (30s poll) and the (currently dead, `PUBLIC_STREAM_ENABLED=false`) SSE `congress.trade` handler both did `totalRows += txs.length` / `totalRows += 1` unconditionally on every response, compounding forever even though the server's `data.total` is a fresh authoritative `COUNT(*)` every time. Fixed: `fetchUpdates` now assigns `totalRows = data.total` (and `filingsImportedToday = data.filingsImportedToday`) whenever the server computes them, never increments; the SSE handler now dedupes by id (`TRADE_BY_ID`) before incrementing and only for rows that pass the active filter. **Bug 2 (filtering didn't change the count):** root cause was that `fetchUpdates()` never sent ANY of the active ticker/member/chamber/side/party filters to `/api/transactions` — it polled+counted the whole unfiltered corpus regardless of what the Trades tab was filtered to. Extracted the filter-building half of `tradesQueryParams()` into a shared `tradesFilterParams()` used by both the page fetch and the poll. Separately, **party chips had ZERO effect on Trades** (visible in the shared toolbar, but only ever wired to `loadTrends()`) — added real party filtering end-to-end: `TxQueryParams.partyBuckets` + `PARTY_BUCKET_SQL_LOCAL` in `delivery/rows.ts` (mirrors `analytics/sql.ts`'s bucketing, kept local per that file's dependency-free convention), `asPartyBuckets` CSV parsing in `delivery/rest.ts` (`/transactions` + `filtersFromQuery` for CSV/RSS), and `partySel`/`partyParam` chip readers in `dashboardHtml.ts` feeding both `tradesFilterParams` and `fetchUpdates`. For the genuinely client-only "Search this page"/Min-Max $ filters (explicitly documented as page-local in the search panel's own copy), `updateTradesCountMsg` now shows "N of M loaded rows match this page filter" instead of implying a corpus-wide count while one is active. **Bug 3 (Trends "TRADES" stat >> Trades tab total):** traced exactly — `analytics/sql.ts` `buildCommonFilters` (used by every Trends aggregate) was missing 3 guards the feed's `buildTxFilters` has always applied unconditionally: `filer_id IS NOT NULL`, the `provider-missing-` synthetic-placeholder exclusion, and the `competitor_backfill`+`EXEC-*`+`COMPETITOR*` orphan-executive-inject exclusion — Trends was counting synthetic/unverified rows the Trades tab has never shown (this is very likely most of the owner-observed 4,102-vs-2,635 gap, given the owner's own hypothesis about Trump/executive competitor-backfill volume). Reconciled by adding the same 3 clauses to `buildCommonFilters` so both surfaces count the identical "real, disclosed trade" universe. Any RESIDUAL difference is a legitimate scope difference (Trends' own time-window + chamber/party chips vs. the Trades tab's own ticker/politician/date/side filters + separate pagination) — added a tooltip on both KPI surfaces (`TRENDS_TRADES_TIP`, and a `title` on `#tradesStats`) so that's self-explanatory instead of an unexplained discrepancy. KEEPOUT (`openRouterVision.ts`) untouched; no `deno.lock` churn committed; no `/api/client/v1/*` files touched (the iOS feed's own separate, still-undocumented-as-unchanged `party`-less `filtersFromQuery` in `client/utils.ts` is unaffected — `docs/client-mobile-api.md`'s existing note about it stays accurate). **Layout-stability follow-up (same branch, second slice):** owner reported (two screenshots) the desktop Trades table renders correctly at first, then "on its own" switches to a broken layout — columns collapse to unreadable ellipsis stubs (DATE→"DA…", TYPE→"T…") and Latency's header wraps one letter per line ("L/A/T/E/N/C/Y"), also "messed up" during a live window resize. Root-caused by building a minimal, faithful standalone repro (served locally, driven with the Browser tool — same CSS/JS extracted verbatim from `dashboardHtml.ts`) rather than guessing: (1) `minColWidth()`'s per-column floors were smaller than the columns' own header labels need (measured via `getBoundingClientRect` against the real production CSS cascade — e.g. "Type↕" needs ~85px, the floor let it get pinned to 44px; "Country↕" needs ~99px, floor was 54px) — confirmed reproducible even in the plain default 6-column view at 1920px, and dramatically worse once an admin session's two extra columns (Imported, Latency) arrive after `/auth/me` resolves (a real async race: `loadMe().then()` re-renders the header + re-triggers `initColumnResize()` after the first, already-correct-looking paint) or a saved localStorage width predates a since-raised floor. (2) Latency's CSS coupled the header (`th.c-latency`) to the same `white-space:normal; word-break:break-word;` its two-line BODY cell legitimately needs — and its accompanying `width/min-width/max-width:55px` was dead code (verified via `getComputedStyle`: in `table-layout:fixed` a `<col>`-level width always wins over a cell's own min/max-width), so nothing actually protected the header once squeezed toward the old 44px floor, and word-break turned the 7-letter label into a vertical letter stack. **Fix:** raised every compact/fixed column's `minColWidth()` floor to its own label's real requirement (Politician/Asset deliberately excluded — they keep the flexible majority via `estimatedColWidth`/`DEFAULT_CAP`, per the prior rebalance intent, confirmed still never ellipsizing full names at ≥1200px); split the CSS so only `td.latency` wraps, the header falls through to the same nowrap+ellipsis every other header gets; added `clampSavedWidth()` — a load-time guard that floors or rejects (null, falls back to natural width) any persisted width against the current `minColWidth`, so a stale/corrupted/negative/zero/non-numeric stored value can never paint even one frame; bumped the persisted-width key `feed-col-widths-v9`→`v10` (old-floor widths are structurally incompatible with the new one, mirrors the prior v8→v9 bump's own reasoning); reconciled `DEFAULT_CAP`'s `type`/`country` soft-caps (were below their own new floor, making them dead weight). Verified via the browser-driven repro across first paint, the admin-arrival race (simulated), a resize sweep 900px→1920px (in steps, including the required 1280/1440/1920 checkpoints), and an adversarial corrupted-storage pass (every column force-set to 5px / -5px) — zero truncation or header wrap in any case. New test (`dashboardHtml.test.ts`): extracts and actually RUNS the real `minColWidth`/`clampSavedWidth` functions (not just string-matching source) against required-floor thresholds and clamp/reject cases, plus CSS/key-bump string assertions. Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; full suite 212 files / 2411 tests green. KEEPOUT (`openRouterVision.ts`) untouched; no `deno.lock` churn. - **2026-08-09 — MONET — IN PR (auto-merge enabled) — SECURITY: Apple JWS x5c full X.509 path validation (`monet/apple-x509-harden`).** Files: `app/src/billing/appleCrypto.ts`, `app/src/billing/appleJws.ts`, `+ __tests__/{appleCrypto,appleJws}.test.ts`, `__tests__/appleCertFixtures.ts` (no prod behavior change to `appleRootCert.ts`). **Exploit closed** (proven by opus verifier PoC against prior code): x5c verification did signature-only chain linking with NO X.509 path validation — `parseCertificate()` stopped at SubjectPublicKeyInfo and never read the v3 extensions block, so nothing distinguished a CA from an end-entity cert; `appleJws.ts` accepted any x5c length 1..5 and only checked signature links + anchored the topmost cert to the pinned root. Anyone holding ANY Apple-issued end-entity cert (a $99 dev/distribution/Developer ID/Apple Pay merchant cert, all of which chain to Apple Root CA - G3) could mint a forged leaf signed by their real Apple cert, present `x5c=[forged_leaf, their_apple_cert, real_intermediate]`, and have an attacker-authored transaction/notification accepted → free Premium via `redeem_apple_purchase` and forged EXPIRED/REVOKE webhooks against any customer's `originalTransactionId`. **Fix (mirrors Apple's app-store-server-library `ChainVerifier`):** (1) `parseCertificate()` now parses the `[3]` extensions block — basicConstraints (`isCa`), keyUsage (`keyCertSign`), extendedKeyUsage, and all extension OIDs (for marker detection); (2) x5c length must be EXACTLY 3 (leaf, intermediate, root); (3) leaf must NOT be `CA:TRUE`, every non-leaf MUST be `CA:TRUE` + keyUsage `keyCertSign`; (4) Apple marker OIDs required — intermediate `1.2.840.113635.100.6.2.1`, leaf `1.2.840.113635.100.6.11.1`; (5) the supplied root (x5c[2]) must BE the pinned Apple Root CA - G3 by SPKI (trust-anchor pin, compared separately); (6) signature links kept, verified top-down against keys we derive (pin→root→intermediate→leaf); (7) validity windows on every cert. Test fixtures gained a full v3-extension DER encoder + per-cert shape overrides; new negative fixtures (all previously accepted, now rejected): the forged-leaf exploit chain, a CA:TRUE leaf, non-CA/keyCertSign-less intermediate, missing marker OIDs, wrong x5c length, expired cert, tampered payload; plus positive controls (valid 3-cert chain verifies; synthetic chain rejected against the REAL embedded Apple root). Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; full suite 211 files / 2397 tests green. KEEPOUT (`openRouterVision.ts`, `dashboardHtml.ts`) untouched. PR: (opening).

Server-parity investigation per param (`app/src/client/utils.ts` `filtersFromQuery`, `app/src/analytics/sql.ts`): **Chamber** (`chamber=`, `asChambers`) is genuinely CSV-capable server-side, so the full multi-selection is forwarded exactly as the web sends it — pill shows full labels joined "+" once modified ("House+Senate"), icon-only "All" at default. **Party** has **no `party=` param at all** on `/api/client/v1/feed` (only Trends analytics accepts one, via `asPartyBucket`, and only a single bucket) — the Trades feed's party filter is therefore entirely client-side (`FeedDashboardView.filteredTrades`, new `PartyFilter.bucket(for:)` mirroring the server's own bucketing), correct for any number of parties selected; Trends analytics sends `party=` only when exactly one party is selected (same single-value fallback the web's own `qSideGroup` chips already use for their case), otherwise showing unfiltered analytics rather than silently guessing one. **Trade Type** (`type=`, `asTxType`) is single-valued server-side — added an Exchange case (`.exchange`, full B/S/E parity with the web's ▲▼⇄ chips) and multi-select `Set<TradeTypeFilter>`; `type=` is forwarded only when exactly one side is selected (mirrors the web's own `selectedSideParam`), and the fetched page is narrowed client-side to the selected subset regardless of count, so 2+ selected sides still filter correctly on-device even though the request itself is unfiltered — documented as the honest constraint it is, not silently dropped. $-pill removal: deleted `AmountThresholdFilter`, `selectedAmountThreshold`/`setAmountThreshold`, the $ `Menu` pill, and all `minAmount` query wiring (`FeedQuery.minAmount`, `exportTransactionsCSV`'s `minAmount` param, the local cache threshold filter) — the unrelated `SubscriptionFilters.minAmount` (alert-subscription filters, different feature) is untouched. Accessibility: every Toggle row carries an explicit accessibilityLabel + accessibilityValue ("Selected"/"Not selected"); every pill's accessibilityLabel spells out the live selection summary; Dynamic Type safe (system fonts, no fixed-width label frames). `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md` updated (Swift-side change only, no server contract change). Gates: `xcodebuild -scheme CongressTrade -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' build` → BUILD SUCCEEDED; `build-for-testing` → TEST BUILD SUCCEEDED. `CongressTradeTests` updated for the renamed `setTradeTypeSelection`/`setPartySelection` APIs and the dropped `TradeTypeFilter.queryValue`/`.all`; added coverage for `PartyFilter.bucket(for:)`, the Trade Type single-vs-multi `type=` behavior, and Chamber's CSV multi-selection — all pass in isolation. Found `testSetTradeTypeSendsTypeQueryParam` already flaky/failing on unmodified `main` before this change (reproduced independently by stashing this work and re-running it): a pre-existing race where `refresh()`'s single feed request and `refreshTrends()`'s ~11 concurrent analytics requests all share one `MockURLProtocol.handler` writing the same captured-URL variable, so an analytics request can win the race and overwrite the feed URL the assertion reads — not introduced by this change; not fixed here (test-infra fix, out of this lane's scope). KEEPOUT respected (`app/src/extraction/openRouterVision.ts`, `app/src/ui/dashboardHtml.ts` untouched). Files: `clients/ios/CongressTrade/{Models.swift,APIClient.swift,Store/CongressTradeStore.swift,Views/Feed/FeedDashboardView.swift}`, `clients/ios/CongressTradeTests/CongressTradeTests.swift`, `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md`. PR: https://github.com/jaywedgeworth22/Congress.Trade/pull/1547 (auto-merge armed, squash).
- **2026-08-09 — MONET — IN PR (auto-merge enabled) — Review-queue false "all done" bug, LANE 1 (`monet/review-queue-resolution`).** Owner: "why did the review queue say all was done, literally daily?" Production measurement: review_queue had 3,497 rows, EVERY ONE resolved=1 (hence the review UI reporting empty/all-done daily); 738 of those resolved filings have ZERO live transactions; 180 filings sit at `filings.ingest_status='needs_review'` despite their queue row claiming resolved (the two stores disagree). **Root cause found**: `autopilot.ts` `resolveEmptyDoc()` (the classifier's "this doc is empty, auto-resolve it" path) flipped `review_queue.resolved=1` for docs the deterministic doc-classifier calls `empty`, but never touched `filings.ingest_status` (left stuck at whatever it was, usually `needs_review`) and recorded no reason anywhere on the review_queue row itself (only a companion `ingestion_decisions` audit row, easy to lose track of) — the exact mechanism behind both measured symptoms. **Fix, migration 0082** (`app/migrations/0082_review_queue_resolution_reason.sql` + mirrored in `admin/migrations.ts`): 3 new `review_queue` columns — `resolution_kind` (published/verified_empty/rejected/orphan_deleted), `resolution_reason`, `resolved_at` — set at every one of the 6 code paths that write `resolved=1` (`normalizer.ts` auto-publish, `agreement.ts` model-agreement publish, `admin/routes.ts` confirm/manual/reject/orphan-filing-deleted, `autopilot.ts` resolveEmptyDoc). **`trg_review_queue_honest_resolution`** (BEFORE UPDATE OF resolved trigger) makes a silent/dishonest resolution structurally impossible going forward, DB-level not just app-discipline: aborts any UPDATE that sets resolved=1 without a recognized resolution_kind, aborts verified_empty/rejected with a blank resolution_reason, and aborts resolution_kind='published' when the doc has zero live transactions at write time — real end-to-end proof in `admin/__tests__/reviewQueueHonestResolution.test.ts` (12 tests against a REAL SQLite DB migrated with the actual .sql files, not a mock) including that it does NOT false-positive on a filing whose transactions were later legitimately superseded by an amendment. Found and fixed a live landmine while wiring the trigger: an existing unconditional `SET resolved=1` data-sweep for `provider_discovered_missing_official` rows (originally PR #1180, living in the migrate-tail mirror) would have aborted on every future `/migrate` (deploy) call once the trigger existed, since `routeProviderOnlyObservationsToReview` (tradeLatency.ts) keeps creating new rows in that category — moved it after the new columns exist and gave it an honest `verified_empty` classification (provider-only placeholder filings have no `raw_object_key`, so they categorically can never gain a live transaction). `resolveEmptyDoc` rewritten to a 2-statement atomic batch: resolves the queue row AND moves `filings.ingest_status` to a new terminal status `'verified_empty'` together, closing the queue/filing desync at its source. **Health check**: extended `src/shared/pipelineHealth.ts` with a `review_resolution_integrity` check (fires `degraded` on resolved rows with no resolution_kind — the 738 case — or needs_review filings with no open queue row — the 180 case); unit tests in `pipelineHealth.test.ts` + a real-seeded-row integration test in `reviewQueueHonestResolution.test.ts` proving it fires on rows shaped exactly like the production incident and stays `ok` on the amendment-supersession non-bug case. Admin `GET /review-queue` now derives an honest per-item `status` from `resolution_kind` (`verified_empty` / `unverified_empty` for legacy rows the backfill couldn't honestly classify, surfacing the exact remaining bad rows instead of a generic "resolved") plus returns `resolutionKind`/`resolutionReason`/`resolvedAt`; dashboard gets a matching "Reopen" action + status-pill color for the new statuses. Migration backfill classifies EXISTING resolved rows from already-true facts only (has a live transaction → published; reason already reads `rejected:...` → rejected) and deliberately leaves genuinely-bad legacy rows (the 738) `resolution_kind IS NULL` — surfaced by the health check for **lane-2 recovery**, not mass-mutated here per the lane split. Gates: `deno check` clean; full suite 213 files / 2427 tests green (+16 new: 12 in the new real-SQLite invariant file, 4 new health-check cases, plus updated `autopilot.test.ts`/`migrations.test.ts` assertions). KEEPOUT (`app/src/extraction/openRouterVision.ts`) untouched; no LLM/OpenRouter/vision calls made or invoked anywhere in this lane — every code path touched is deterministic SQL. Metrics/invariant enforced: `resolved=1` now requires either ≥1 live transaction (`published`) or an explicit non-blank `resolution_reason` (`verified_empty`/`rejected`), enforced by `trg_review_queue_honest_resolution`; `filings.ingest_status` and `review_queue.resolved` kept consistent in one place per write path. PR: (opening).

- **2026-08-09 — MONET — MERGED (`162163b2`) — Apple backend: Sign in with Apple + StoreKit 2 IAP, entitlement Stripe-OR-Apple.** PR #1553 merged (auto-merge, squash), CI green (typecheck+test, gitleaks). Migration 0080/0081 + code are on `main`; NOT yet applied to the production D1 (that's `POST /api/admin/migrate`, an ADMIN_TOKEN-gated production-intent step this session didn't have the token for and didn't self-authorize — additive/idempotent/non-breaking whenever it runs, and the whole feature stays inert either way since `APPLE_SIGNIN_ENABLED`/`APPLE_IAP_ENABLED` are both unset). Branch `monet/apple-backend` off `origin/main`. Fully env-gated off (`APPLE_SIGNIN_ENABLED`, `APPLE_IAP_ENABLED` both unset by default) so nothing activates before App Store Connect setup. Investigated prior art first: PR #1345 (merged) already shipped a minimal `POST /billing/apple/confirm` (decode-only, no signature verification) that writes Apple state directly onto the Stripe-shaped `users` columns — left untouched (still the fallback path iOS's existing `SubscribeView.swift` calls); this lane adds the recommended path alongside it. **Crypto core** (`app/src/billing/appleCrypto.ts` + `appleJws.ts` + `appleRootCert.ts`): hand-rolled minimal ASN.1 DER/X.509 parser + ECDSA chain verifier (no npm X.509 lib, so it typechecks under both the Cloudflare Worker and the Deno runtime this app dual-targets — same constraint `admin/access.ts`'s hand-rolled JWKS+RS256 verifier already works under) that verifies an Apple ES256 x5c JWS chain (leaf → intermediate → Apple Root CA - G3) with NO network call — the real root cert (fetched directly from `https://www.apple.com/certificateauthority/AppleRootCA-G3.cer`, byte-verified against the download, SHA-256 fingerprint documented in the file) is pinned in `appleRootCert.ts`. Test fixtures build a synthetic root→intermediate→leaf chain and sign real ES256 JWS entirely in-process via WebCrypto (`billing/__tests__/appleCertFixtures.ts`) — no `openssl` subprocess, no committed key material; caught and fixed a real double-DER-wrapping bug in the fixture builder itself during development (SPKI export already returns the complete structure). **Sign in with Apple**: `POST /auth/apple` (`auth/appleIdentity.ts` — RS256-against-Apple's-published-JWKS verification, ~1h cache, mirrors `admin/access.ts`'s pattern without sharing code since the trust boundaries are unrelated) accepts `{identityToken, nonce?, fullName?}`, verifies iss/aud(bundle id)/exp/nonce, issues the SAME session (`auth/session.ts`) the Google flow issues. `auth/users.ts` `upsertUserFromApple`: keyed by stable Apple `sub`; links to an existing account on a VERIFIED email match (mirrors the Google callback's account-takeover guard — an unverified email never links AND never names a new account either, falls back to a synthetic placeholder — caught by a test that found the original draft would UNIQUE-collide on a victim's existing email). Migration `0080_apple_signin.sql` (`users.apple_sub`, partial-unique index). **IAP**: `redeem_apple_purchase` client command (`client/commands.ts`, follows the existing command idempotency model) verifies the client's `signedTransaction` JWS via the pinned-root chain verifier, validates bundleId + productId (env-overridable via `APPLE_PRODUCT_MONTHLY`/`APPLE_PRODUCT_ANNUAL`, falling back to the hardcoded `trade.congress.premium.{monthly,annual}` defaults), upserts an `apple_subscriptions` ledger row (migration `0081_apple_iap.sql`) keyed on `originalTransactionId` — idempotent (restore-purchases = call again), refuses to reassign a transaction id already owned by a different account (409-equivalent, not a silent takeover). `POST /api/webhooks/apple` (`billing/appleWebhook.ts`, mounted before `publicApiGuard`'s bot/UA guard via a new `/api/webhooks` EXEMPT_PREFIXES entry — Apple's servers carry no browser-shaped UA) handles App Store Server Notifications V2's minimal set (DID_RENEW/EXPIRED/REVOKE/DID_CHANGE_RENEWAL_STATUS), each verifying its OWN nested `signedTransactionInfo`/`signedRenewalInfo` JWS independently; idempotent on `notificationUUID` via a new claim/release/processed ledger (`appleWebhookEvents.ts`) mirroring the Stripe webhook's exact pattern (`billing/webhookEvents.ts`) without touching it. **Entitlement OR-logic** (`billing/entitlement.ts` `resolveEntitlementAsync`/`isPremiumUserAsync`, new — the existing pure sync `entitlementOf`/`isPremiumUser` are UNCHANGED, so Stripe's own resolution code was never restructured): premium = Stripe-active (unchanged) OR an active `apple_subscriptions` row. Wired into every entitlement-bearing read (`GET /auth/me`, `GET /billing/status`, `GET /api/client/v1/bootstrap`, `GET /api/client/v1/me`) and every Premium-gated write (CSV export, alert webhook/SSE subscription create/activate, PDF download, `subscriptionOwnerEntitled` delivery-time recheck) — found and fixed 2 existing test doubles (`freemiumGating.test.ts`, `auth/__tests__/routes.test.ts`) whose overly-permissive fake `DB.prepare` would have silently returned "yes, Apple-premium" for the new ledger query regardless of SQL text. `Entitlement` gained one optional additive field, `source: 'stripe'|'apple'|null`, for the client to pick the right "manage subscription" surface. **Migrations**: registered in BOTH the file-based (`app/migrations/0080_*.sql`, `0081_*.sql`) and the admin-tail (`admin/migrations.ts` `APPLE_SIGNIN_SCHEMA_STATEMENTS`/`APPLE_IAP_SCHEMA_STATEMENTS`, spliced into `POST_0024_SCHEMA_STATEMENTS` which `POST /api/admin/migrate` already spreads) paths per repo convention, plus the `admin/__tests__/migrations.test.ts` schema-parity snapshot updated — that test independently confirmed applying all `.sql` files end to end produces byte-identical resulting schema to the admin tail. Also added the 5 new env knobs to the `GET /api/admin/config-sources` registry (`auth-billing` category). Docs: `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md` (full request/response contract, entitlement semantics, restore-purchases idempotency) + `app/docs/mobile-app-roadmap.md` (status note) updated. **Not built** (explicitly out of scope / owner-only): actually creating the two subscription products in App Store Connect, enabling the Sign in with Apple capability + key config, setting the env knobs — noted in the PR body. iOS client integration is a separate lane; this PR ships the backend contract only. Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean (Deno check, both runtimes); full suite 211 files / 2382 tests green, ~95 of them new (crypto chain verification incl. tamper/expiry/wrong-root/broken-link negative cases, Apple identity token verification incl. nonce/issuer/audience/expiry negatives, user linking incl. the account-takeover guard, the full command execution branch incl. idempotent restore + owner-mismatch conflict + env-configured product ids, the full webhook handler incl. duplicate-delivery idempotency + all 4 notification types + unknown-transaction + bundle-mismatch, and the Stripe-OR-Apple resolver incl. short-circuit-without-querying-Apple-when-Stripe-already-premium). Gates: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; all `npm test` 151 files / 1636 tests green across two consecutive full runs (one unrelated pre-existing SSE-deadline timing test flaked once under full-suite load, passed on both isolated and full-suite reruns — untouched by this diff).
- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Delivery/client input hardening, command lifecycle, honest tx_type, detail privacy+budget (2026-07-19) — PR OPENED.** Branch `claude/delivery-client-v3`. Scope confined to `app/src/delivery/`, `app/src/client/`, `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md` per assignment; verified against current main first (PR #592's SSE→WebSocket-DO work touched only `clients/pwa/`, not `app/src/delivery`; PR #620's resource-governors work touches `deadLetter.ts`/`outbox.ts`/`webhook.ts`/`targetCircuit.ts`, disjoint from this PR's files — no overlap either way). Seven items: (1) fractional/invalid `?limit=`/`?offset=`/export `maxRows` now floored before the `<=0`/cap clamp in `buildTransactionsQuery`/`buildTransactionsExportQuery` (`rows.ts`) — was already mid-flight in the worktree's one dirty file, finished + tested (NaN/Infinity/fractional cases). (2) Zero-delta `?since=` polls (both `/api/transactions` and `/api/client/v1/feed`) now skip the full `COUNT(*)` + today-filings aggregate entirely, omitting `total`/`filingsImportedToday` rather than lying with `0` — verified both known clients (dashboard `fetchUpdates()`, PWA `poll()`) already gate every read of those fields behind "got new rows back?" and no-op otherwise, so this is a pure cost cut with no client-visible behavior change. (3) `POST /api/client/v1/commands` idempotency is now race-safe: a true concurrent-duplicate INSERT collision (`DuplicateCommandError` on the unique `(user_id, idempotency_key)` index) replays the winner instead of 500ing, and a `queued`/`running` row whose owning request died mid-flight is reclaimed + re-run after `STALE_IN_FLIGHT_COMMAND_TTL_MS` (2 min) instead of being replayed forever. (4) `tx_type` NULL passthrough (also mid-flight in the dirty file, finished): `mapTransaction` no longer defaults a malformed/undisclosed side to `'P'`; confirmed downstream aggregates (`tickerSummarySql`/`memberSummarySql`), `matchesFilters`' `sides` filter, and `csvCell` all already handle a non-matching/null `tx_type` correctly, and the legacy dashboard's `txToRow()` already has its own `tx.txType || 'P'` display fallback — no client regression. (5) `GET /filings/:docId` (public, previously unauthenticated AND unbudgeted) now applies the same per-IP daily row budget as the feed and returns `toPublicFiling()` instead of the raw internal `Filing` (was leaking `rawObjectKey` R2 key, `extractor`/`modelVersion` model slugs, and raw `error` text to anonymous callers); `/api/client/v1/{trade/:id,ticker/:ticker,member/:memberIdOrName}` also gained the row-budget check `/feed` already had. (6) `resolveMember` (client) and the feed's `memberName` filter (delivery, shared code path) now escape `%`/`_`/`\` before building the `LIKE` pattern (new `escapeLikePattern` in `rows.ts`, paired with `ESCAPE ''` in the SQL) so a literal wildcard character in a search term is matched literally, not as a SQL wildcard. (7) "Premium CSV export accepts bearer, not just cookie" — verified against main: PR #558 ("remove all column gating and CSV export premium limits") deleted the entire premium gate on `/api/export/transactions.csv`, including the cookie-only `getCurrentUser()` check the item describes — the endpoint is now fully public with NO auth check of any kind, so the described bug no longer exists to fix (confirmed via `git show 02b78cf`, which shows the deleted gate literally used `getCurrentUser`, not `getCurrentUserFromRequest`). No code change for #7 beyond a comment correcting stale "premium" language; flagged in the PR body in case reinstating a premium-gated export is wanted as an explicit, separate product decision (must use `getCurrentUserFromRequest` this time). Added ~30 new tests across `rows.ts`/`queries.ts`/`routes.ts`. Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm test` 1697/1697 own-scope tests green across 3 full-suite runs — the only flakes observed were 1-4 timeouts in `src/admin/__tests__/reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` (untouched by this PR), which passes 19/19 in isolation every time (confirmed twice), i.e. pre-existing machine-load flakiness, not a regression.
- 2026-07-19 — CLAUDE — In Progress (PR #653, squash auto-merge armed) — **Agreement-fingerprint text normalization** (branch `claude/agreement-fingerprint-normalization`). Production evidence: the agreement cascade was reading documents successfully but had 0 publishes in 6+ hours (324/324 candidates `cascade_unresolved`). Root cause: `materialRowFingerprint`/`sameRowSet`/`buildMajorityRows` in `app/src/extraction/agreement.ts` compared every row field — including free text like `assetName`/`description` — through byte-strict `canonicalText` (trim+uppercase+whitespace-collapse only), so cross-vendor formatting drift ("First Data Corp." vs "FIRST DATA CORP") blocked agreement on filings every model actually read correctly. Fix: the 9 strict/enum fields (ticker, amounts, tx_type, tx_date, owner, is_option, cap_gains_over_200, filing_status) keep byte/value equality always; the 7 free-text fields now compare via a new `canonicalAgreementText` — casefold + punctuation-strip + the EXISTING `cleanAssetString` company-suffix canonicalizer (no new normalizer invented) — one comparator shared across tier-1/2 unanimity and tier-3 majority voting, with near-miss agreement preferring the higher-confidence contributing model's own text. Kill switch `AGREEMENT_TEXT_NORMALIZATION` (default true). Picked up substantial prior work already in the worktree from an earlier interrupted attempt (reviewed, sound, matched spec); found and fixed 2 wiring gaps left from that attempt (admin `/config-sources` diagnostics registry + `.dev.vars.example` both missing the new var vs every sibling `AGREEMENT_*` tunable) and one deterministic test-data bug (a tier-3 test used `ticker: null`, which routes row-grouping through `arbitrationRowKey`'s pre-existing, untouched, out-of-scope raw-text fallback instead of the new comparator — fixed by giving the test rows a ticker). Gates: typecheck clean; all 8 agreement-related test files green (73/73); full suite 1677/1685 — the 8 failures are `reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` timeouts, verified pre-existing/unrelated by reproducing identically against clean `main` with this diff fully reverted (fleet-wide host-load contention, not a regression).

- 2026-07-17 — MONET — In Progress — **Telemetry failsafe: circuit breaker + move usage-telemetry outbox off D1** (branch `monet/telemetry-failsafe-off-d1`, owner-directed, prevents recurrence of the usage.jays.services 2-day-OOM D1 overage incident, ~$1,153). Adds a durable CONFIG_KV circuit breaker (consecutive-failure threshold, exponential backoff 30s→30min capped, half-open probe on the next natural attempt) around `deliverUsageTelemetryEvent` in `app/src/shared/thirdPartyTelemetry.ts`; the queue consumer (`app/src/index.ts` `handleIngestMessage`/`handleDeadLetterMessage`) now routes new `usage.telemetry` events straight to the R2 outbox (no live delivery attempt, acks instead of retrying) while the circuit is open. Stops writing new rows to D1 `usage_telemetry_fallback_events` entirely — R2 is now the sole durable outbox for new events; a bounded one-time drain (read+delete, no re-churn UPDATE) empties pre-existing legacy D1 rows and a CONFIG_KV marker skips the D1 SELECT once observed empty. R2 outbox is now capped (`USAGE_TELEMETRY_FALLBACK_MAX_OBJECTS`, default 5000, drops new writes at cap) and TTL-expired (`USAGE_TELEMETRY_FALLBACK_TTL_DAYS`, default 14d, checked during flush). `flushUsageTelemetryFallback` skips listing R2/querying D1 entirely while the circuit is open. All limits env-overridable (see `app/src/shared/types.ts`). Added/updated tests in `thirdPartyTelemetry.test.ts`, `thirdPartyTelemetryQueue.test.ts`, `admin/__tests__/batchStatusUsage.test.ts` (updated 4 tests that relied on the old D1-fallback-of-last-resort behavior to instead exercise the R2 fallback). Gates green: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, full `npm test` 132 files / 1392 tests pass. Migration/table left in place per instructions (no drop in this PR — future cleanup). Committed locally, NOT pushed/PR'd/deployed — owner gates landing per task brief. #agent-sync claim posted (KEEPOUT on thirdPartyTelemetry.ts + index.ts usage.telemetry routing until this lands).

Now, when initial parsing or markdown-fenced string extraction fails, `extractJsonFallback` serves as a syntax-aware safety net to gracefully recover malformed, truncated, or conversational outputs. - **OpenRouter Response Healing Integration (AG) — DEPLOYED / LIVE VERIFIED 2026-07-16 via PR #506.** Enabled OpenRouter's `response-healing` plugin in `openRouterVision.ts` to automatically fix malformed JSON responses (missing brackets, trailing commas, markdown wrappers) directly on the provider side before hitting our parser. This drastically reduces expensive and unnecessary model failovers triggered by minor syntax errors. PR #506 merged, all tests passed, and canonical `ship.sh` deployed the Worker to production. Live-verified by reprocessing 2 recent House docs on production via `/api/admin/reprocess` which successfully parsed with Mistral OCR and returned 4 extracted rows perfectly matching the database with 0 errors.

- **Ingestion fetch outage: R2 known-length regression fix + dead-letter recovery (CLAUDE, M) —
  2026-07-12.** Every filing fetch in production failed from 2026-07-11T19:14Z onward with
  `fetcher: Provided readable stream must have a known length` — PR #284's `limitedFilingBody`
  size-guard wraps the response in a NEW JS ReadableStream, which R2 `put()` rejects (no known
  length; upstream Content-Length doesn't carry over, and OGE's Domino server sends chunked with
  no Content-Length at all). Casualties: all 500 filings of a just-started H-2015 house backfill
  (ingestion_outbox rows dead-lettered to `failed` after the 5-cycle cap) + all 17 executive OGE
  278-Ts discovered by the first post-#315 watcher poll (still cycling, self-recover on fix).
  The fetcher unit tests mocked `RAW_FILES.put` as stream-draining, so CI could not catch it.
  Fix: `bufferFilingBody()` buffers through the existing byte-count guard (25MB cap intact) and
  hands R2 a known-length `Uint8Array`; regression test pins a no-Content-Length chunked response
  (put receives buffered bytes, never a bare stream). Recovery: new
  `POST /api/admin/ingest-requeue-failed` (`requeueFailedIngestionOutbox`: failed→pending with
  fresh dead-letter budget, optional docIdPrefix, dryRun) + the per-minute outbox flush drains the
  backlog. Gates: typecheck + 111 files / 977 tests green. Playbook: merge → `deploy.yml` →
  requeue failed rows → verify fetches resume (receipts in #agent-sync closeout).

- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Monitor-informed budget throttle: `GET /api/budget-status` self-throttle feedback loop (2026-07-19) — PR OPENED, branch `claude/ct-budget-throttle`.** New `app/src/shared/monitorBudgetGate.ts`: a bounded (default 5s, hard-capped 15s), isolate-memory-cached (default 2min TTL) reader for the API Usage Monitor's cross-app `GET /api/budget-status`, bearer-auth via `USAGE_MONITOR_READ_TOKEN` (falls back to `USAGE_MONITOR_INGEST_TOKEN`, mirroring the monitor's own read/ingest-token convention). Wired as an early, non-blocking advisory-defer check into `handleAutopilotTick` (`app/src/extraction/autopilot.ts`) — the backlog-autopilot *discretionary* drain only, never the real-time per-filing ingestion path — deferring a doc (existing `skipReasons`/`outcomes` pattern) only when EVERY provider in its resolved model trio is reported at/over budget. Composes strictly UNDER PR #620's hard LLM-USD governor once that lands; does not touch `bakeoff.ts`/`configuredVision.ts`/`openRouterVision.ts`. **FAILS OPEN on any error, timeout, non-2xx, malformed body, or missing config — never throttles when it can't get a clean answer** (explicitly tested: unreachable, timeout, 401, malformed JSON, unconfigured provider, disabled switch). New env knobs (`app/src/shared/types.ts` + `app/docs/config-registry.md`): `USAGE_MONITOR_READ_TOKEN`, `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_ENABLED` (default on), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_THRESHOLD` (default 1.0 = monitor's "exceeded"), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (default 120000), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 5000, capped 15000). Gates: 17/17 new `monitorBudgetGate.test.ts` cases green, 18/18 existing `autopilot.test.ts` green, `npm run typecheck` clean (after `npm ci` refreshed a stale `node_modules` — installed `@jaywedgeworth22/congress-trading-shared` was v1.8.3 vs the v1.10.0 commit pinned in package.json, the exact stale-install symptom flagged fleet-wide in #agent-sync today; unrelated to this change). No KEEPOUT collision found in #agent-sync history on `monitorBudgetGate.ts`/`autopilot.ts`/`shared/types.ts`. Not merging — leaving PR green/ready for owner review per branch-protection.

- 2026-07-19 — CLAUDE — In Progress (PR #627, auto-merge armed) — **Backlog autopilot + doc classifier + self-healing provider routing + metadata-grounded prompts** (branch `claude/extraction-autopilot`). Picked up a prior agent's completed-but-uncommitted implementation from the `extraction-autopilot` worktree (WIP was sitting uncommitted on top of an old `main` base): cron-gated queue-driven backlog drain through the existing agreement cascade (pilot runs default 50 docs, 1 attempt/model/doc, error-class halt at 2 repeats via `AUTOPILOT_ERROR_CLASS_HALT_THRESHOLD`, park-with-receipt + `POST /api/admin/autopilot/acknowledge`); pre-extraction `filings.doc_class` classifier (deterministic signals first, one cheap enum-constrained OpenRouter call only for ambiguous scans); per-`provider:model` circuit breaker with cost-guarded runtime substitution (`providerHealth.ts`); metadata-grounded extraction prompts (`KNOWN DOCUMENT FACTS` block + anti-hallucination guardrail, prompt version bumped to `stock-act-ptr-v3-grounded`). Merged current `main` in (8 commits behind), resolving migration-number conflicts by renumbering the new migrations from `0047`/`0048` to `0049`/`0050` (main had since taken `0047_subscription_quota_active_only.sql`/`0048_retention_indexes.sql` via #606/#607) and reconciling the `admin/migrations.ts` schema-tail array + parity test. **PR #620** (resource governors incl. `shared/llmSpend.ts` global daily-USD ceiling) has **not merged to main yet** (open, CI green, auto-merge armed but `mergeStateStatus: DIRTY`/conflicting) — composition with it was therefore descoped; the autopilot's own `autopilot_budget` meter is a feature-scoped allocation (like the existing `AGREEMENT_DAILY_LLM_BUDGET`), and since autopilot routes every model call through the same `handleAgreementCheck`/`runCandidateOnDoc` choke point as the per-minute cron, it will automatically inherit `#620`'s global ceiling with zero code changes once that PR lands. Fixed 3 real post-merge bugs surfaced by the full test run: a stale `EXTRACTION_PROMPT_VERSION` assertion in an unrelated pre-existing test, a `pdf-lib` content-stream-compression blind spot in the doc-classifier's own test fixture (text-layer byte-sniff can never see Flate-compressed streams — replaced with a hand-written uncompressed fixture matching `ingestion/classifier.test.ts`'s existing precedent), and a wall-clock-vs-injected-clock mismatch in `providerModelBanRetryAfter` (added an optional `now` param mirroring its sibling `recordProviderHealth`). Gates: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; all `npm test` 151 files / 1636 tests green across two consecutive full runs (one unrelated pre-existing SSE-deadline timing test flaked once under full-suite load, passed on both isolated and full-suite reruns — untouched by this diff).

Shared pin bumped v1.8.x → v1.10.0 (`904ea96`, adds `openrouterRequestEnrichment`/`buildCallClassifier` + optional `providerRequestId` on `UsageTelemetryEventSchema`; 1.9.0's `normalizeCompanyName` removal verified safe — CT uses its local `app/src/shared/companyName.ts`). `openRouterVision.ts` `callOpenRouter` now spreads classifier enrichment (top-level deterministic per-doc `user`, flat `trace` — no `metadata` sub-object, `undefined` never ``) into the request body, degrading to an unenriched request on any enrichment error (paid call never blocked); captures the OpenRouter response `id` as `providerRequestId` on results AND billed-error paths, threaded through `bakeoff.ts`/`telemetry.ts`/`orchestrator.ts`/`thirdPartyTelemetry.ts` onto pushed usage-telemetry events (idempotency-key basis untouched). Tests added for enrichment shape, determinism, degradation, id capture/omission, and telemetry threading. KEEPOUT: `app/src/extraction/openRouterVision.ts` until this lands.
- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Delivery/client input hardening, command lifecycle, honest tx_type, detail privacy+budget (2026-07-19) — PR OPENED.** Branch `claude/delivery-client-v3`. Scope confined to `app/src/delivery/`, `app/src/client/`, `app/docs/client-mobile-api.md` per assignment; verified against current main first (PR #592's SSE→WebSocket-DO work touched only `clients/pwa/`, not `app/src/delivery`; PR #620's resource-governors work touches `deadLetter.ts`/`outbox.ts`/`webhook.ts`/`targetCircuit.ts`, disjoint from this PR's files — no overlap either way). Seven items: (1) fractional/invalid `?limit=`/`?offset=`/export `maxRows` now floored before the `<=0`/cap clamp in `buildTransactionsQuery`/`buildTransactionsExportQuery` (`rows.ts`) — was already mid-flight in the worktree's one dirty file, finished + tested (NaN/Infinity/fractional cases). (2) Zero-delta `?since=` polls (both `/api/transactions` and `/api/client/v1/feed`) now skip the full `COUNT(*)` + today-filings aggregate entirely, omitting `total`/`filingsImportedToday` rather than lying with `0` — verified both known clients (dashboard `fetchUpdates()`, PWA `poll()`) already gate every read of those fields behind "got new rows back?" and no-op otherwise, so this is a pure cost cut with no client-visible behavior change. (3) `POST /api/client/v1/commands` idempotency is now race-safe: a true concurrent-duplicate INSERT collision (`DuplicateCommandError` on the unique `(user_id, idempotency_key)` index) replays the winner instead of 500ing, and a `queued`/`running` row whose owning request died mid-flight is reclaimed + re-run after `STALE_IN_FLIGHT_COMMAND_TTL_MS` (2 min) instead of being replayed forever. (4) `tx_type` NULL passthrough (also mid-flight in the dirty file, finished): `mapTransaction` no longer defaults a malformed/undisclosed side to `'P'`; confirmed downstream aggregates (`tickerSummarySql`/`memberSummarySql`), `matchesFilters`' `sides` filter, and `csvCell` all already handle a non-matching/null `tx_type` correctly, and the legacy dashboard's `txToRow()` already has its own `tx.txType || 'P'` display fallback — no client regression. (5) `GET /filings/:docId` (public, previously unauthenticated AND unbudgeted) now applies the same per-IP daily row budget as the feed and returns `toPublicFiling()` instead of the raw internal `Filing` (was leaking `rawObjectKey` R2 key, `extractor`/`modelVersion` model slugs, and raw `error` text to anonymous callers); `/api/client/v1/{trade/:id,ticker/:ticker,member/:memberIdOrName}` also gained the row-budget check `/feed` already had. (6) `resolveMember` (client) and the feed's `memberName` filter (delivery, shared code path) now escape `%`/`_`/`\` before building the `LIKE` pattern (new `escapeLikePattern` in `rows.ts`, paired with `ESCAPE ''` in the SQL) so a literal wildcard character in a search term is matched literally, not as a SQL wildcard. (7) "Premium CSV export accepts bearer, not just cookie" — verified against main: PR #558 ("remove all column gating and CSV export premium limits") deleted the entire premium gate on `/api/export/transactions.csv`, including the cookie-only `getCurrentUser()` check the item describes — the endpoint is now fully public with NO auth check of any kind, so the described bug no longer exists to fix (confirmed via `git show 02b78cf`, which shows the deleted gate literally used `getCurrentUser`, not `getCurrentUserFromRequest`). No code change for #7 beyond a comment correcting stale "premium" language; flagged in the PR body in case reinstating a premium-gated export is wanted as an explicit, separate product decision (must use `getCurrentUserFromRequest` this time). Added ~30 new tests across `rows.ts`/`queries.ts`/`routes.ts`. Gates: `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm test` 1697/1697 own-scope tests green across 3 full-suite runs — the only flakes observed were 1-4 timeouts in `src/admin/__tests__/reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` (untouched by this PR), which passes 19/19 in isolation every time (confirmed twice), i.e. pre-existing machine-load flakiness, not a regression.
- 2026-07-19 — CLAUDE — In Progress (PR #653, squash auto-merge armed) — **Agreement-fingerprint text normalization** (branch `claude/agreement-fingerprint-normalization`). Production evidence: the agreement cascade was reading documents successfully but had 0 publishes in 6+ hours (324/324 candidates `cascade_unresolved`). Root cause: `materialRowFingerprint`/`sameRowSet`/`buildMajorityRows` in `app/src/extraction/agreement.ts` compared every row field — including free text like `assetName`/`description` — through byte-strict `canonicalText` (trim+uppercase+whitespace-collapse only), so cross-vendor formatting drift ("First Data Corp." vs "FIRST DATA CORP") blocked agreement on filings every model actually read correctly. Fix: the 9 strict/enum fields (ticker, amounts, tx_type, tx_date, owner, is_option, cap_gains_over_200, filing_status) keep byte/value equality always; the 7 free-text fields now compare via a new `canonicalAgreementText` — casefold + punctuation-strip + the EXISTING `cleanAssetString` company-suffix canonicalizer (no new normalizer invented) — one comparator shared across tier-1/2 unanimity and tier-3 majority voting, with near-miss agreement preferring the higher-confidence contributing model's own text. Kill switch `AGREEMENT_TEXT_NORMALIZATION` (default true). Picked up substantial prior work already in the worktree from an earlier interrupted attempt (reviewed, sound, matched spec); found and fixed 2 wiring gaps left from that attempt (admin `/config-sources` diagnostics registry + `.dev.vars.example` both missing the new var vs every sibling `AGREEMENT_*` tunable) and one deterministic test-data bug (a tier-3 test used `ticker: null`, which routes row-grouping through `arbitrationRowKey`'s pre-existing, untouched, out-of-scope raw-text fallback instead of the new comparator — fixed by giving the test rows a ticker). Gates: typecheck clean; all 8 agreement-related test files green (73/73); full suite 1677/1685 — the 8 failures are `reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` timeouts, verified pre-existing/unrelated by reproducing identically against clean `main` with this diff fully reverted (fleet-wide host-load contention, not a regression).

- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Monitor-informed budget throttle: `GET /api/budget-status` self-throttle feedback loop (2026-07-19) — PR OPENED, branch `claude/ct-budget-throttle`.** New `app/src/shared/monitorBudgetGate.ts`: a bounded (default 5s, hard-capped 15s), isolate-memory-cached (default 2min TTL) reader for the API Usage Monitor's cross-app `GET /api/budget-status`, bearer-auth via `USAGE_MONITOR_READ_TOKEN` (falls back to `USAGE_MONITOR_INGEST_TOKEN`, mirroring the monitor's own read/ingest-token convention). Wired as an early, non-blocking advisory-defer check into `handleAutopilotTick` (`app/src/extraction/autopilot.ts`) — the backlog-autopilot *discretionary* drain only, never the real-time per-filing ingestion path — deferring a doc (existing `skipReasons`/`outcomes` pattern) only when EVERY provider in its resolved model trio is reported at/over budget. Composes strictly UNDER PR #620's hard LLM-USD governor once that lands; does not touch `bakeoff.ts`/`configuredVision.ts`/`openRouterVision.ts`. **FAILS OPEN on any error, timeout, non-2xx, malformed body, or missing config — never throttles when it can't get a clean answer** (explicitly tested: unreachable, timeout, 401, malformed JSON, unconfigured provider, disabled switch). New env knobs (`app/src/shared/types.ts` + `app/docs/config-registry.md`): `USAGE_MONITOR_READ_TOKEN`, `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_ENABLED` (default on), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_THROTTLE_THRESHOLD` (default 1.0 = monitor's "exceeded"), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (default 120000), `USAGE_MONITOR_BUDGET_STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 5000, capped 15000). Gates: 17/17 new `monitorBudgetGate.test.ts` cases green, 18/18 existing `autopilot.test.ts` green, `npm run typecheck` clean (after `npm ci` refreshed a stale `node_modules` — installed `@jaywedgeworth22/congress-trading-shared` was v1.8.3 vs the v1.10.0 commit pinned in package.json, the exact stale-install symptom flagged fleet-wide in #agent-sync today; unrelated to this change). No KEEPOUT collision found in #agent-sync history on `monitorBudgetGate.ts`/`autopilot.ts`/`shared/types.ts`. Not merging — leaving PR green/ready for owner review per branch-protection.

- **[Congress.Trade][CLAUDE] Enrichment/prices reliability bundle — PR #652 OPENED, auto-merge armed (2026-07-19).** Picked up and finished a nearly-complete branch (`claude/enrichment-prices-v3`). Seven items: (1) enrichment transient-failure retry — a thrown provider error now writes an attempt-aged `transient-retry:<n>:<iso>` backoff marker (1h→24h doubling cap) instead of tombstoning; only a clean all-providers "no data" result still tombstones. (2) SEC EDGAR ticker-map fetch failures are no longer cached as a permanent empty `Map` (was silently blinding every subsequent lookup for the isolate's life). (3) price refresh aborts the rest of the run on an auth/plan/rate-limit error (401/402/403/429) instead of burning the remaining daily budget on daily calls guaranteed to fail identically. (4) every attempted FMP call (success or throw) now counts against the shared daily meter, matching real billing. (5) `spx_at_trade`/`spx_at_filing` now `COALESCE` onto the prior value on conflict so an incomplete same-run `spx_eod` fetch never regresses a good anchor to null. (6) `selectTickersNeedingPrices` now orders oldest-traded-first (`cursor_seq ASC`) so new trades can't perpetually crowd out an unpriced backlog. (7) not-found backoff is now two-stage (7d first miss, 30d second+/stalled) — fixed a downstream consistency bug this uncovered in `admin/routes.ts`s `marketPending`, which only excluded `price_unavailable=1` under one cutoff and would have over-reported "pending" for stage-2/stalled tickers. Also added: `jobs.ts` now reserves a 20%-of-cap price-refresh budget floor (gated on an FMP key being configured) so enrichment (which runs first on the shared daily FMP counter) can't starve price refresh to zero. No new migration (`price_unavailable` already a plain unconstrained INTEGER). Gate: typecheck clean; full suite 150/151 files, 1693/1698 tests green — the only 5 failures are pre-existing `Miniflare`/`workerd` timeouts in `reviewResolutionD1.test.ts` (documented elsewhere in this log as fragile under resource-constrained hosts; zero references to any file this PR touches, reproduced in isolation under this host's very high concurrent multi-agent load). Added/completed test coverage for all seven items.

Modified `.github/workflows/deploy-deno.yml` to trigger deployments on a 3-hour cron schedule (`0 */3 * * *`) rather than on every `push`. This batches code changes together into fewer deployments to save Deno Deploy compute quota.
- **2026-07-27** - `[Congress.Trade]`: Deployment Deduplication Fix (AG) — COMPLETED. Modified `.github/workflows/deploy-deno.yml`'s python pre-check script to interrogate the GitHub Actions API for the most recent successful run of the deploy workflow. If the `head_sha` of the last successful run identically matches the current `GITHUB_SHA` being triggered by the cron, the workflow exits early and skips deploying to Deno Deploy. This robustly ensures compute time is never burned if there are no code updates.
- **2026-07-27** - `[Congress.Trade]`: Deno Deploy Free Tier Cost Profile Tuning (AG) — COMPLETED. This ensures maximum headroom under the Deno Deploy free tier usage caps.

- **2026-07-30 — KIMI — COMPLETED — PROD 502 INCIDENT (congress.trade down, Uptime-Robot): root cause + durable fix.** Root cause: host-local Caddyfile (`/etc/usage-monitor/Caddyfile`, bind-mounted into `oracle-caddy-1`) reverse-proxied congress.trade to the per-deploy timestamped container name (`congress-app-congress-trade-050455830214:5000`); every Coolify (re)deploy mints a new container name → routing broke on each deploy. (4) host.jays.services (Coolify UI, also 502) pointed at tailscale IP `100.97.154.2:8000` unreachable from inside the container → now `reverse_proxy coolify:8080` over the shared `coolify` network. - **2026-08-02 — MONET — IN PROGRESS (PR #1238) — AG-takeover verification + production actually deployed.** Owner directed "make sure everything is resolved well and keep on working" after AG completed 6 audit slices. **CRITICAL CORRECTION: AG's sync-3 "merged and deployed, ship.sh prod deploy verified live" was wrong — the six PRs (#1228 #1229 #1230 #1231 #1232 #1233) were merged but NOT deployed.** `ship.sh` does not deploy (Coolify does, on push to main; that webhook had not fired), so it health-checked the OLD image, migrated it, and printed success. Evidence twice, fresh/uncached, `via: 1.1 Caddy`: no `Secure` on either OAuth cookie, no `strict-transport-security` at all, `ct_auth_origin` still `http://congress.trade` — the exact value #1228 changed. This is audit **P1-5** exactly as predicted. **RESOLVED: triggered a real Coolify deploy (`m11v3kxwocsx33os6cfgsxew`); P0-3 now genuinely live** — `__Host-ct_auth_origin=https%3A%2F%2Fcongress.trade; HttpOnly; Secure` + `strict-transport-security: max-age=31536000`. Also confirmed P0-4 live by executing `fmtCompany` from the deployed bundle (`MICROSOFT CORP` → `Microsoft Corp.`). **FLEET NOTE: Coolify API works at `https://host.jays.services` with `COOLIFY_AGENTS` ONLY with a browser User-Agent — Cloudflare 403s every other agent on every path and it looks exactly like an auth failure.** PR #1238 (adversarial review of AG's merged work, 15 agents, findings verified then re-refuted): (1) **deploy revision receipt** — `/api/health` reports `build.sha`, `ship.sh` asserts it BEFORE migrating and fails on mismatch, ending this class of false "deployed"; (2) **migrate would have failed the next deploy** — the list rebuilds a superseded strict UNIQUE index that a later statement drops, throwing `UNIQUE constraint failed` when a deprecated row shares `(doc_id,source,row_key)` with a live one (harmless while migrate swallowed errors, fatal now #1230 fails closed); (3) **`tx_cursor_seq` unindexed on `tx_id`** — both #1232 repair statements AND the trigger itself (runs on EVERY INSERT) correlate on it → full scan of one-row-per-transaction-ever (~76k+); repair was O(poisoned × 76k) in an HTTP request and ingestion degrades quadratically; (4) **cursor trigger swap silently drops trades** — `DROP`+`CREATE` on the same name with no enclosing transaction leaves a window with NO trigger; an INSERT there gets `cursor_seq = NULL`, invisible to EVERY feed read (`cursor_seq > ?` applies even at `since=0`) and unhealable by a repair keyed on `>= 1e12` (NULL >= 1e12 is NULL); reopened every deploy. Fixed via create-`_v2`-then-drop-v1 + NULL-healing repair + readiness accepting either name (else health 503s between deploy and migrate); verified on real SQLite, idempotent across two runs; (5) readiness postcondition **failed open** when the DB was unreachable — the exact case it exists to catch; (6) `ship.sh` used `curl -fsS`, discarding migrate's entire `failed[]` diagnostic on 500; (7) 3 tracked files still carried `SQLITE_WEB_PASSWORD:-admin` (applying them resurrects it) — removed + `*.orig`/`*.rej` gitignored; (8) the 25 rewritten `.py` scripts failed SILENTLY on a missing password (empty password → login HTML returned as data) — now raise; (9) new **CI guard** blocking fail-open secret defaults, password literals, and unauthenticated `sqlite_web` — verified it catches all three originals at `173a3ba8`; (10) shared-package pin check could **pass by doing nothing** (`|| true` swallowed git's error exit); (11) **P1-15** dead GA tag removed — prod injected `G-B3J0XHK0FX` while CSP is `script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'`, so it never loaded once; guard test asserts every external `<script src>` origin is actually allowed by the CSP the same module emits. Every guard mutation-tested. Gates: `deno check` clean, **2025/2025**, merged origin/main (#1237/#1239). STILL OPEN/UNCLAIMED from the audit: P0-2 health-depth, P1-2 KV prefix leak (session tokens in the browsable SQLite DB; fix logs all users out — needs owner timing), P1-8 CSV entitlement (product decision), P1-16 webhook replay window, P1-17 queue retention, P1-18 migration drift, P2 UI/a11y + docs drift, iOS P0-6/7/8.
